The construction, interpretation, validity, enforceability and effect of all provisions of this Amendment including, but not limited to, the payment of the Obligations and the legality of the interest rate and other charges shall be construed and enforced in accordance with the internal laws of the Commonwealth of Massachusetts (without regard to conflicts of laws). Borrower agrees to submit to non-exclusive personal jurisdiction in the Commonwealth of Massachusetts in any action or proceeding arising out of this Amendment and, in furtherance of such agreement, Borrower hereby agrees and consents that, without limiting other methods of obtaining jurisdiction, personal jurisdiction over Borrower in any such action or proceeding may be obtained within or without the jurisdiction of any court located in the Commonwealth of Massachusetts, and that any process or notice of motion or other application to any such court in connection with any such action or proceeding may be served upon Borrower by registered or certified mail to or by personal service at the last known address of Borrower, whether such address be within or without the jurisdiction of any such court.</font></div></div>

BORROWER AND LENDER HEREBY KNOWINGLY, VOLUNTARILY AND INTENTIONALLY WAIVE THE RIGHT TO A TRIAL BY JURY IN RESPECT OF ANY LITIGATION BASED HEREON ARISING OUT OF, UNDER OR IN CONNECTION WITH THIS AMENDMENT OR ANY OTHER LOAN DOCUMENTS (AS DEFINED EITHER IN THE CREDIT AGREEMENT) CONTEMPLATED TO BE EXECUTED IN CONNECTION HEREWITH, OR ANY COURSE OF CONDUCT, COURSE OF DEALINGS, STATEMENTS (WHETHER VERBAL OR WRITTEN) OR ACTIONS OF ANY PARTY. THIS WAIVER CONSTITUTES A MATERIAL INDUCEMENT FOR THE LENDER TO ACCEPT THIS AMENDMENT.</font></div></div>

Each of the counterparts shall constitute but one in the same instrument and shall be binding upon each of the parties individually as fully and completely as if all had signed but one instrument.</font></div></div>

Borrower and SSSHT each hereby further acknowledges and agrees that, as of the Eighth Amendment Date, the Loan Documents, as amended by this Amendment, are not subject to any defenses, rights of setoff, claims or counterclaims that might limit the enforceability thereof, the obligations created and evidenced thereby or the terms and provisions thereof.</font></div></div>

This Amendment is only a modification of the Original Agreement and is not intended to, and shall not be construed to, effect a novation of the Original Agreement, or to constitute a modification of, or a course of dealing at variance with, the Original Agreement (each as amended by this Amendment), such as to require further notice by Lender to require strict compliance with the terms of the Original Agreement in the future.</font></div></div>

  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:9.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.223%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.593%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">The Borrower and Lender are parties to that certain Amended and Restated Credit Agreement dated as of the Original Closing Date (as defined herein) (as may have been further amended and supplemented from time to time, the &#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Original Credit Agreement</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d;), which Original Credit Agreement is hereby amended, restated and superseded (except to the extent the provisions of the Original Credit Agreement expressly survive termination thereof) in its entirety by this Agreement as follows:</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.25pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:37.653%;text-indent:-0.136%;padding-left:0.027%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:42.667%;text-align:center;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">ARTICLE I. </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">DEFINITIONS</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.4pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.224%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">SECTION 1.01</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Defined Terms</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">. As used in this Agreement, the following terms have the meanings specified below:</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.4pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.224%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.05pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">ABR</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">,&#x201d; when used in reference to any Loan (e.g., an &#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">ABR Loan</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d;) or Borrowing (e.g., an</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">ABR Borrowing</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d;), refers to whether such Loan or Borrowing, is bearing interest at a rate determined</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">by reference to the Adjusted Base Rate.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.45pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.182%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.065%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Acquisition Cost</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, with respect to a Funded Property, the Contract Sales Price to be  paid for such Funded Property plus actual out-of-pocket expenses that are paid to unrelated third parties  in connection with the closing of such acquisition, including without limitation, attorneys&#x2019; fees, recording fees, documentary stamps, real estate brokers&#x2019; commissions and real estate tax adjustments incident to  the closing of such sale, all as evidenced on a settlement or closing statement delivered to, and approved by, Lender.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.2pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.223%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.593%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Acquisition Fee Allocation</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, with respect to any Funded Property, the acquisition fee payable to SSSHT Advisor or its Affiliates (including, if applicable, SSSHT Property Manager and/or SSSHT Dealer Manager) in accordance with the Private Placement Memorandum after repayment in full of the corresponding Loan.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.3pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.224%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Entity</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means each Additional Property Owner, each Additional Property OpCo</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">and each Intermediate Entity (if applicable) with respect to such Additional Property Owner and/or</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Property OpCo.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:7.773%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Loan</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; has the meaning set forth in </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Section 2.01(b)</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">.</font></p>

  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.225%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3.05pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.62%;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Loan Amortization Schedule</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; shall mean, for each Additional Loan, the</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">amortization schedule set forth in the applicable Credit Agreement Supplement.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.206%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.38%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Loan Documentation</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, with respect to each Additional Loan, the applicable</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Credit Agreement Supplement, Fee Subordination Agreement, any amendments to this Agreement or any</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">other Loan Documents executed in connection therewith and any other documents evidencing, securing</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">or governing such Additional Loan.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.225%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.62%;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Loan Maturity Date</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; shall mean, for each Additional Loan, the Additional Loan</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Maturity Date set forth in the applicable Credit Agreement Supplement.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.221%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.574%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Management Agreement</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means a property management agreement by and</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">between an Additional Property Owner and the property manager with respect to an Additional Property</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">reasonably acceptable to Lender, as may be amended from time to time as permitted by this Agreement.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.45pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.221%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.574%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Property</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means a real property which is indirectly acquired by SSSHTOP using</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">the proceeds of an Additional Loan, which shall be satisfactory to Lender in its sole and absolute</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">discretion.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.221%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.574%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Property OpCo</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means a single purpose Delaware limited liability company, or</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">any successors thereto, in each case, in the capacity of non-owner operator of an Additional Property</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">permitted by this Agreement, in each case, as consented to by Lender in its sole discretion.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.224%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Additional Property Owner</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means a single purpose Delaware limited liability company, or</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">any successors thereto, in each case, in the capacity of owner of an Additional Property permitted by this</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Agreement, in each case as consented to by Lender in its sole discretion.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:9.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.203%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.333%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Adjusted Base Rate</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means an interest rate per annum </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">per </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">equal to the greater of (a) the Base Rate plus the Base Rate </font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Applicable </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Margin</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">, or</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> and</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">(b) the</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> applicable</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">SOFR Rate Margin. Any change in the Adjusted Base Rate shall be effective immediately from and after a change in the </font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Adjusted </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Base Rate (or the Federal Funds Effective Rate, as applicable).</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.35pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.226%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.63%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Adjusted Daily Simple SOFR</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; or &#x201c;</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Adjusted Daily Simple SOFR Rate</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means a rate per annum equal to the sum of (a) Daily Simple SOFR plus (b) the SOFR Rate Margin.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.4pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.221%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.574%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Adjusted Term SOFR</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, with respect to any Term SOFR Loan for any Interest Period, the sum of (i) the Credit Spread Adjustment for the relevant Interest Period</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">, </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">(ii) the </font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">applicable</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">SOFR Rate Margin and (iii) the greater of (a) the Floor and (b) the forward-looking term rate for a period comparable to such Available Tenor based on SOFR that is published by CME Group Benchmark</font></p>
  <div style="flex-direction:column;display:flex;padding-bottom:0.86in;min-height:0.99in;justify-content:flex-end;box-sizing:border-box;"><p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p><p style="margin-left:1pt;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:12pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">2</font></p><p style="margin-left:1pt;font-size:10pt;margin-top:7.8pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p></div>

  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:3.05pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.565%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Administration Ltd (&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">CBA</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d;) (the sum of (i) and (iii)</font><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">, </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;Term SOFR</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; or &#x201c;Term SOFR Rate</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d;) and displayed on CME&#x2019;s Market Data Platform (or other commercially available source providing such quotations as may be selected by Lender from time to time taking into account any evolving or then prevailing market convention for determining such source), at approximately 6:00 a.m. New York City time, two Business Days (the &#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Lookback Day</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d;) prior to the commencement of such Interest Period; provided that if by 5:00 pm (New York City time) on any Lookback Day, any tenor of Term SOFR for such day has not been published, then such tenor of Term SOFR for such day will be such tenor of Term SOFR as published in respect of the first preceding SOFR Business Day for which such rate was published so long as such first preceding SOFR Business Day is not more than three SOFR Business Days prior to such Lookback Day.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.05pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.222%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.583%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Affiliate</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, with respect to a specified Person, another Person that directly, or indirectly through one or more intermediaries, Controls or is Controlled by or is under common Control with the Person specified.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.35pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:7.773%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:22.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Affiliate Prepared Information</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; has the meaning set forth in </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Section 3.12</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">. &#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Agreement</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; has the meaning set forth in the Preamble.</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.224%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.15pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Applicable Margin</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means with respect to any (a) Base Rate Loan, the Base Rate Margin and</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">(b</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">) SOFR Loa</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">n</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">, t</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">h</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">e SO</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">F</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">R Rate Margin.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.4pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.22%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.556%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Appraisal</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; (whether one or more) means a written appraisal of a Funded Property by an MAI appraiser reasonably satisfactory to Lender; provided that if the Appraisal is satisfactory to Fannie Mae, it shall be deemed to be satisfactory to Lender.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.35pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>

  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.4pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.225%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.62%;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Base Rate Margin</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means three hundred (300) basis points</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;font-style:italic;min-width:fit-content;">plus </font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">any applicable Increase</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Adjustment</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">.</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.221%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.6pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.574%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Benchmark</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, initially, (i) SOFR, and (ii) if a Benchmark Transition Event and its related Benchmark Replacement have occurred with respect to SOFR or the then-current Benchmark, then &#x201c;Benchmark&#x201d; means the applicable Benchmark Replacement to the extent that such Benchmark Replacement has replaced such prior benchmark rate pursuant to </font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Section 2.11(c)</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.3pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.188%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.148%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Benchmark Replacement</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, with respect to any Benchmark Transition Event, the sum of: (i) the alternate benchmark rate that has been selected by Lender and Borrower giving due consideration to (A) any selection or recommendation of a replacement benchmark rate or the mechanism for determining such a rate by the Relevant Governmental Body or (B) any evolving or then-prevailing market convention for determining a benchmark rate as a replacement to the then-current Benchmark for</font></p>
  <div style="margin-left:1.065%;margin-top:0;justify-content:flex-start;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.667%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:3.5063425481376655%;display:inline-flex;justify-content:flex-start;">U.S.</font><div style="width:100%;display:inline;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">dollar-denominated syndicated credit facilities and (ii) the related Benchmark Replacement Adjustment; provided that, in each case, if such Benchmark Replacement as so determined would be less than the Floor, such Benchmark Replacement will be deemed to be the Floor for the purposes of this Agreement and the other Loan Documents.</font></div></div>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.05pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.221%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.565%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Benchmark Replacement Adjustment</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, with respect to any replacement of the then-current Benchmark with an Unadjusted Benchmark Replacement for any applicable Available Tenor, the spread adjustment or method for calculating or determining such spread adjustment (which may be a positive or negative value or zero), that has been selected by Lender and Borrower giving due consideration to (a) any selection or recommendation of a spread adjustment, or method for calculating  or determining such spread adjustment, for the replacement of such Benchmark with the applicable Unadjusted Benchmark Replacement by the Relevant Governmental Body or (b) any evolving or then-prevailing market convention for determining a spread adjustment, or method for calculating or determining such spread adjustment, for the replacement of such Benchmark with the applicable Unadjusted Benchmark Replacement for U.S. dollar-denominated syndicated or bilateral credit facilities.</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.203%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.333%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Benchmark Replacement Conforming Changes</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means, with respect to any Benchmark Replacement, any technical, administrative or operational changes (including changes to the definition of &#x201c;Base Rate,&#x201d; the definition of &#x201c;Business Day,&#x201d; or &#x201c;SOFR Business Day&#x201d;, the definition of &#x201c;Interest Period&#x201d; or any similar or analogous definition (or the addition of a concept of &#x201c;interest period&#x201d;), timing and frequency of determining rates and making payments of interest, timing of borrowing requests or prepayment, conversion or continuation notices, length of lookback periods, the applicability of any &#x201c;breakage&#x201d; provisions and other technical, administrative or operational matters) that Lender decides  may be appropriate to reflect the adoption and implementation of such Benchmark Replacement and to permit the administration thereof by Lender in a manner substantially consistent with market practice (or, if Lender decides that adoption of any portion of such market practice is not administratively feasible or if Lender determines that no market practice for the administration of such Benchmark Replacement exists, in such other manner of administration as Lender decides is reasonably necessary in connection with the administration of this Agreement and the other Loan Documents).</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.45pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:9.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.224%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Benchmark Replacement Date</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means the earliest to occur of the following events with respect to the then-current Benchmark or any published component used in the calculation thereof:</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;"> </font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.224%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">(a) in the case of clause (a) or (b) of the definition of &#x201c;Benchmark Transition Event&#x201d;, the later of (i) the date of the public statement or publication of information referenced therein and (ii) the</font></p>
  <div style="flex-direction:column;display:flex;padding-bottom:0.8in;min-height:0.93in;justify-content:flex-end;box-sizing:border-box;"><p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p><p style="margin-left:1pt;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:12pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">4</font></p><p style="margin-left:1pt;font-size:10pt;margin-top:7.8pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p></div>
  <hr style="page-break-after:always;">
  <div style="min-height:0.96in;"><p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p></div>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:3.05pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.593%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">date on which the administrator of such Benchmark (or the published component used in the calculation thereof) permanently or indefinitely ceases to provide all Available Tenors of such Benchmark (or such component thereof); or</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.35pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <div style="margin-left:1.065%;text-indent:7.194%;font-size:0;margin-top:0;justify-content:flex-start;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.269%;min-width:6.667%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:1.3493707990094506%;text-indent:0;display:inline-flex;justify-content:flex-start;">(a)</font><div style="width:100%;display:inline;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">in the case of clause (c) of the definition of &#x201c;Benchmark Transition Event&#x201d;, the first date on which such Benchmark (or the published component used in the calculation thereof) has been determined and announced by the regulatory supervisor for the administrator of such Benchmark (or such component thereof) to be no longer representative; provided, that such non-representativeness will be determined by reference to the most recent statement or publication referenced therein in such clause (c) and even if any Available Tenor of such Benchmark (or such component thereof) continues to be provided on such date.</font></div></div>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.15pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.223%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.593%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">For the avoidance of doubt, (i) if the event giving rise to the Benchmark Replacement Date occurs on the same day as, but earlier than, the Reference Time in respect of any determination, the Benchmark Replacement Date will be deemed to have occurred prior to the Reference Time for such determination and (ii) the &#x201c;Benchmark Replacement Date&#x201d; will be deemed to have occurred in the case of clause (a) or (b) with respect to any Benchmark upon the occurrence of the applicable event or events set forth therein with respect to all then-current Available Tenors of such Benchmark (or the published component used in the calculation thereof).</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.15pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.224%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.602%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Benchmark Transition Event</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means the occurrence of one or more of the following events with respect to the then-current Benchmark:</font></p>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.4pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <div style="margin-left:1.065%;text-indent:7.215%;font-size:0;margin-top:0;justify-content:flex-start;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.537%;min-width:6.667%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:1.3493707990094506%;text-indent:0;display:inline-flex;justify-content:flex-start;">(a)</font><div style="width:100%;display:inline;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">a public statement or publication of information by or on behalf of the administrator of such Benchmark (or the published component used in the calculation thereof) announcing that such administrator has ceased or will cease to provide all Available Tenors of such Benchmark (or such component thereof), permanently or indefinitely, provided that, at the time of such statement or publication, there is no successor administrator that will continue to provide any Available Tenor of such Benchmark (or such component thereof);</font></div></div>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.2pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <div style="margin-left:1.065%;text-indent:7.215%;font-size:0;margin-top:0.05pt;justify-content:flex-start;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.537%;min-width:6.667%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:1.3493707990094506%;text-indent:0;display:inline-flex;justify-content:flex-start;">(b)</font><div style="width:100%;display:inline;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">a public statement or publication of information by the regulatory supervisor for the administrator of such Benchmark (or the published component used in the calculation thereof), the Federal Reserve Board, the Federal Reserve Bank of New York, an insolvency official with jurisdiction over the administrator for such Benchmark (or such component), a resolution authority with jurisdiction over the administrator for such Benchmark (or such component) or a court or an entity with similar insolvency or resolution authority over the administrator for such Benchmark (or such component), which states that the administrator of such Benchmark (or such component) has ceased or will cease to provide all Available Tenors of such Benchmark (or such component thereof) permanently or indefinitely, provided that, at the time of such statement or publication, there is no successor administrator that will continue to provide any Available Tenor of such Benchmark (or such component thereof); or</font></div></div>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:9.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <div style="margin-left:1.065%;text-indent:7.215%;font-size:0;margin-top:0;justify-content:flex-start;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.537%;min-width:6.667%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:1.3493707990094506%;text-indent:0;display:inline-flex;justify-content:flex-start;">(c)</font><div style="width:100%;display:inline;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">a public statement or publication of information by the regulatory supervisor for the administrator of such Benchmark (or the published component used in the calculation thereof) announcing that all Available Tenors of such Benchmark (or such component thereof) are no longer, or as of a specified future date will no longer be, representative.</font></div></div>
  <p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.3pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.223%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.593%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">For the avoidance of doubt, a &#x201c;Benchmark Transition Event&#x201d; will be deemed to have occurred with respect to any Benchmark if a public statement or publication of information set forth above has</font></p>
  <div style="flex-direction:column;display:flex;padding-bottom:0.8in;min-height:0.93in;justify-content:flex-end;box-sizing:border-box;"><p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p><p style="margin-left:1pt;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0.5pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#ff0000;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:line-through;font-size:12pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">5</font></p><p style="margin-left:1pt;font-size:10pt;margin-top:7.8pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p></div>
  <hr style="page-break-after:always;">
  <div style="min-height:0.96in;"><p style="font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p></div>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3.05pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.62%;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">occurred with respect to each then-current Available Tenor of such Benchmark (or the published component used in the calculation thereof).</font></p>
  <p style="text-indent:4.286%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3.05pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.667%;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Benchmark Transition Start Date</font><font style="color:#010101;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d; means in the case of a Benchmark Transition Event, the earlier of (i) the applicable Benchmark Replacement Date and (ii) if such Benchmark Transition Event is a public statement or publication of information of a prospective event, the 90th day prior to the expected date of such event as of such public statement or publication of information (or if the expected date of such prospective event is fewer than 90 days after such statement or publication, the date of such statement or publication).</font></p>
  <p style="margin-left:1.107%;text-indent:7.184%;font-size:10pt;margin-top:4.55pt;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;margin-right:6.093%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201c;</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">Benchmark Unavailability Period</font><font style="color:#0000ff;white-space:pre-wrap;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11pt;font-family:Times New Roman;min-width:fit-content;">&#x201d;
